# Etherwise — Proof Points & Capabilities

_Compiled 2026-08-14 04:43 IST · 1661 agency + 1283 cross-client facts (2906 trusted)._

> Agency-level knowledge extracted from client calls but NOT specific to one client. Cited; use CONFIRMED / CROSS-VERIFIED for outreach.

## Agency capabilities & method

### architecture
- A browser/Chrome extension (one-click) is being used that adds tasks via API, attaches the email thread as a PDF, auto-generates a subject-based case name, and remembers the selected list until a different one is chosen. [CONFIRMED · fathom/163774252 @ 00:22:03 – 00:23:00]
- A client matching tool was built that uses notes, preferences, and AI-based best matching to return best and lowest matches for connecting clients with technicians/service providers. [CONFIRMED · fathom/156775218 @ 00:21:25]
- A dual-agent system is being designed: one agent writes prompts and a second agent verifies those prompts against established guidelines. [CONFIRMED · fathom/132222000 @ 00:03:00 - 'एक एजेंट जो प्रॉम्ट लिख रहा है और दूसरा एज़ेंट जो यह गाइड्लाइन्स के अगेंस वेरिफाय कर रहा है']
- A future enhancement discussed is a browser-native tool (not Gmail-specific) that can directly add emails or data from multiple sources (e.g., Gmail, Notion) into the tracking system. [CONFIRMED · fathom/163774252 @ 00:23:00]
- A template base with multiple tables is created and replicated across clients with minimal customization [CONFIRMED · https://www.loom.com/share/b2c27a5953d54088a4a50b2553522dbd @ 02:00 - 'template base that we have created with all the different tables and everything that we need. And now we are just replicating it for the clients']
- Abhi built a commission/salary calculation script in Airtable that computes closer bookings, applies commission percentages, calculates monthly salaries, and sends them out via email — with no third-party platform interaction. [CONFIRMED · fathom/132450295 @ there are things like calculate... how many bookings came in, what is the commission for each closer... calculate what salary is based on that... preparing the salaries and sending those out via email]
- Abhi built a script-based automation that listens for Stripe payment webhooks, then uses an e-signature API to populate and send contracts, and sends a follow-up email notification to the payer. [CONFIRMED · fathom/132450295 @ whenever there is a person who... makes a payment... we have a webbook that watches for like, you know, any Stripe... then we want to send them maybe the offer letter or the contract letter]
- Abhi implements Airtable automations including: Calendly webhook-to-Airtable record capture, interface button triggers (e.g., send offer letter, send contract), date-based email reminders, and Make.com scenarios for third-party integrations not natively supported in Airtable. [CONFIRMED · fathom/132450295 @ 00:06:35 - 00:08:02]
- Abhi uses UML/entity relationship diagrams in draw.io to architect Airtable base structures (tables, link records, lookup fields, rollup fields) before implementation, and documents these for client review before building. [CONFIRMED · fathom/132450295 @ I always have that habit of like, you know, architecting stuff before implementing anything... I kind of use maybe a draw IO is the tool that I use a lot]
- Abhi's performance optimization approach for large Airtable bases includes offloading transactional or non-critical data to Google Sheets (linked via Airtable record ID) to stay within Airtable's 500,000 records per table limit. [CONFIRMED · fathom/132450295 @ on the Airtable baseline, there is like 500,000 records per table per base limit... why not create a Google Sheet with the Airtable record ID and maintain those details there?]

ManyChat integrates with Make.com and Airtable for lead logging. Airtable would be your database for the leads and then we'll use Make.com to glue ManyChat with the Airtable.']
- Abhijeet Gandhi noted that the planned power-dialer/transfer functionality is achievable with the voice agents already built by the agency. [CONFIRMED · fathom/100449673 @ 00:10:51]
- Abhijeet Gandhi prioritizes which processes to automate by calculating hours saved per team member per day and multiplying across the month (e.g. 5 people × 1 hour/day = 150 hours/month saved). [CONFIRMED · fathom/171448316 @ five people... putting in an hour daily doing certain tasks... five hours a day that is 150 hours that I'm going to save within a month]
- Abhijeet Gandhi's proposal methodology: 2-3 onboarding calls with the operational team to map current processes, followed by a detailed architecture diagram and documentation covering what to automate, the pipeline design, tooling, and estimated hours — all delivered before any build begins. [CONFIRMED · fathom/171448316 @ I usually have two to three onboarding calls with the actual team... then I go ahead and prepare the architecture diagram, the documentation... you have a complete plan before you say yes, or I start working on anything]
- Abhijeet advised against finalizing tools on the discovery call; the approach is to first understand all pain points and business processes, then recommend the right tools and architecture. [CONFIRMED · fathom/149957674 @ 00:17:00 – 'I'm not trying to finalize any tools in these calls, I would understand all the requirements much better.']
- Abhijeet explained that API-based integration is more reliable than UI scraping because the API remains stable even when the UI changes, whereas UI automation breaks on interface upgrades. [CONFIRMED · fathom/140347413 @ the API is always more reliable... because the API never changes even if the UI is changing]
- Abhijeet noted that Claude has native connectors to OneDrive, enabling it to access files stored there directly. [CONFIRMED · fathom/140347413 @ directly have connectors in Cloud to connect with OneDrive so you can access the information as it needs]
- Abhijeet proposed a normalization/fairness mechanism where instead of filling one client's bucket entirely before moving to the next, leads are distributed in small interleaved batches (e.g. 25 to client A, 25 to client B, repeat) to ensure all clients receive leads even if total volume is lower than expected. [CONFIRMED · fathom/155692198 @ 00:11:34]
- Abhijeet proposed that Claude could access the Roam database directly (bypassing the Roam UI/API) using database queries, since Roam is itself a UI layer over a database. [CONFIRMED · fathom/140347413 @ we can talk to the developer and just get access to the database... Claude can access directly the database and not having to deal with the Roam]
- ActiveCampaign 'add to list' API operation is implemented as a list subscription call, and 'remove from list' is implemented as an unsubscribe call [CONFIRMED · https://www.loom.com/share/136353b42093425db2becb83e3859149 @ when we say add to the list, there is no particular API to add to list.
